# CR subrule coverage

Generated by `tests/cr-citations/cr-subrule-coverage.test.ts` (CR audit tool 1.17) from the CR `v26.03` rules below section level (not examples), the `it()` titles in `tests/**/*.test.ts` (not `tests/jnet`), and the owners in `tests/cr-citations/cr-section-owners.json` (tool 1.13). Do not edit by hand; regenerate with `UPDATE_CR_SUBRULE_COVERAGE=1 npx vitest run tests/cr-citations/cr-subrule-coverage.test.ts`.

A title cites a rule when it names that exact number, with or without `CR `, or one of its examples (`1.12.2.ex1` cites 1.12.2). Uncited subrules are in `tests/baselines/cr-subrules.json` under the same owner, and that list may only shrink. The titles are a snapshot from the last regeneration; the test checks the owners and which subrules are cited.
